9. 放弃（释放家族串行锁）：本卡还没有任何推送到远端 `main` 的 C 组提交（状态文件没有 `PUSHED_SHA`），而外部前置长期不可用或用户决定改期时，可以放弃本卡：本地已有未推送的 C 组提交时先 `libra reset --mixed HEAD^` 撤销它；再用 `libra restore` 逐个撤销本卡写集内产品文件与版本面文件的改动、删除本卡新建的未跟踪文件（只限本卡允许集合内的路径）；`plan-status.md` 不整文件还原，只把本计划行改回；然后把 `Lifecycle / Acceptance` 改回 `pending` / 空并写明原因，同步 `plan-status.md` 本计划行，以 `PHASE=abandon` 运行暂存守卫，执行 `libra commit -S -s -m "docs(plan): abandon $CARD (#1056)"`，以 `PHASE=abandon` 运行提交后范围检查与计划目录推送脚本。「没有推送过」不只看可丢弃的本地状态文件：`PHASE=abandon` 的暂存守卫先 fetch，并向 GitHub 核对状态文件里的 `C_COMMIT`（若有）不在远端 `main` 上、`origin/main` 的版本仍等于当前 `HEAD`（撤销后的基线）的版本、下一 patch 版本的候选分支 `release/v<下一版本>` 与 tag `v<下一版本>` 都不存在；任一不满足（例如推送已经落地而状态文件还没写下 `PUSHED_SHA`）就拒绝放弃。已经推送过的卡不能放弃，只能按第 6 步修复或前滚。

**运行状态文件函数（以下各脚本内联同一段；`CARD` 必须已导出）：**

```bash
STATE_DIR=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE="$STATE_DIR/1056-${CARD:?export CARD first}.env"
state_get() { if [ -f "$STATE" ]; then awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$STATE"; fi; }
state_put() { mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; touch "$STATE"; awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") != 1' "$STATE" > "$STATE.tmp"; printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$STATE.tmp"; mv "$STATE.tmp" "$STATE"; }
```

**记录 C_COMMIT（`bash` 运行；C 组提交与提交后范围检查之后运行，rebase 恢复的 amend 之后再运行一次）：**

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
STATE_DIR=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE="$STATE_DIR/1056-${CARD:?export CARD first}.env"
state_get() { if [ -f "$STATE" ]; then awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$STATE"; fi; }
state_put() { mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; touch "$STATE"; awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") != 1' "$STATE" > "$STATE.tmp"; printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$STATE.tmp"; mv "$STATE.tmp" "$STATE"; }
gpgsig_digest() { libra cat-file -p "$1" | awk '/^gpgsig /{ f = 1; print; next } f && /^ /{ print; next } f { exit }' | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1; }
sha=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
test -n "$sha"
previous=$(state_get C_COMMIT)
if [ -n "$previous" ] && [ "$previous" != "$sha" ] && [ "$(gpgsig_digest "$sha")" = "$(gpgsig_digest "$previous")" ]; then
  echo "HEAD carries the signature of $previous: libra rebase copies gpgsig verbatim and the copy is no longer valid; run libra commit --amend -S --no-edit first" >&2
  exit 1
fi
state_put C_COMMIT "$sha"
echo "C_COMMIT=$sha"
```

**开工检查（`bash` 运行）：** 整个工作区（含未跟踪文件）必须干净；`HEAD` 只允许快进到 `origin/main`；本卡登记的 `COMPATIBILITY.md` 行号上必须是预期的命令行（按行首前缀在全文恰好命中一次且位于该行号）。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) allowed='src/command/write_tree.rs tests/command/write_tree_test.rs docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/README.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='240'; c_phase=yes; row_file='docs/development/commands/README.md'; row_prefix='| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
compat_prefix() {
  case "$1" in
    240) printf '%s\n' '| write-tree | partial | Writes the index out as a nested tree object' ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}
check_compat_rows() {
  local file="$1" n prefix hits
  for n in $compat_lines; do
    prefix=$(compat_prefix "$n")
    hits=$(grep -nF -- "$prefix" "$file" | cut -d: -f1 | tr '\n' ' ')
    if [ "$hits" != "$n " ]; then echo "COMPATIBILITY.md row $n is not the expected row in $file (prefix found at: $hits)" >&2; return 1; fi
  done
}
porcelain=$(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all)
if [ -n "$porcelain" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" >&2; echo "worktree is not clean; this card needs exclusive use of the checkout" >&2; exit 1; fi
libra fetch origin
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
head=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
if [ "$head" != "$remote_main" ]; then
  libra pull --ff-only
  head=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
fi
test "$head" = "$remote_main"
check_compat_rows COMPATIBILITY.md
```

**工作区独占检查（`bash` 运行，`MODE` 取 `check`、`snapshot` 或 `verify`）：** 暂存区必须为空；工作区里每个改动或未跟踪的路径都必须在本卡允许集合内。`snapshot` 另把 `HEAD`、路径集合与每个改动文件的 SHA-256（本文件除外）写入 `/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state/1056-<CARD>.tree`；`verify` 重新计算并要求完全相同。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) allowed='src/command/write_tree.rs tests/command/write_tree_test.rs docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/README.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='240'; c_phase=yes; row_file='docs/development/commands/README.md'; row_prefix='| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
case "${MODE:?export MODE=check, snapshot or verify}" in check|snapshot|verify) ;; *) echo "unknown MODE=$MODE" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
allowed_all="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1 $plan $status_file"
porcelain=$(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all)
staged=$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3 { x = substr($0, 1, 1); if (x != " " && x != "?") print substr($0, 4) }')
if [ -n "$staged" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$staged" >&2; echo "the index has staged entries; unstage them with 'libra restore --staged <path>' and rerun" >&2; exit 1; fi
paths=$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort)
for path in $paths; do
  case " $allowed_all " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "change outside this card's write set: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
tree_file="/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state/1056-$CARD.tree"
fingerprint() {
  local path
  printf 'HEAD %s\n' "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)"
  for path in $paths; do
    if [ "$path" = "$plan" ]; then continue; fi
    if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
  done
}
case "$MODE" in
  snapshot) mkdir -p /Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state; fingerprint > "$tree_file" ;;
  verify) test -s "$tree_file"; now=$(fingerprint); test "$now" = "$(cat "$tree_file")" ;;
esac
echo "worktree exclusive for $CARD ($MODE)"
```

**C 组运行器（`bash` 运行，`CARD` 为本卡 ID）：** 从本文件按下列固定锚点抽出脚本，所以执行的就是本文件里经过审查的门块，不靠执行者逐段复制。抽取规则：锚点在全文（或标注为本卡范围时在本卡 `Task` 标题到下一个 `Task` 标题之间）必须恰好出现一次，取它之后的第一个 `bash` 代码块，锚点与代码块之间不得隔着标题。Verification 门块按顺序拼成一个门块脚本，开头是 `set -eo pipefail`，块与块之间插入 `set +u`，在同一个 shell 里执行，所以前置块定义的函数可供后面的门块使用；C 组 fail-closed 脚本单独成一个脚本，在门块脚本通过之后以新的 `bash` 进程运行，门块里 TC 的 `export`（`LIBRA_BIN`、`TC_*`、`GIT_*` 等）因此不会带进 C 组的全量测试。任何一条命令失败，所在脚本立即非零退出，运行器随之停止。锚点只在正文里查找：代码块、「评审结论与修订记录」节与「实施证据」节都不参与匹配，所以脚本自身、日志与收据里出现同样的文字不会造成歧义。各卡的门块锚点及顺序就是下面脚本里 JSON 配置的 `gates`（`card` 表示只在本卡范围内查找），C 组脚本的锚点是 `c`。有 C 组提交的卡，门块脚本先跑 bump 前窗口检查、版本面内容检查（脚本开头导出 `VSRC=worktree`）与有网站面的卡的精确句预检，再跑本卡的 Verification 门块，所以这三项也由通过标记绑定，漏跑会被暂存守卫发现。运行器只接受 `MODE` 为 `normal`（缺省）或 `repair`，并原样传给门块脚本；工作区独占检查用的 `MODE=snapshot`、`MODE=verify` 由运行器在调用时单独设置。`no_c` 列出的卡没有 C 组提交，运行器拒绝它们，它们的门块只由 `PHASE=accept` 的暂存守卫重跑。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
state_dir=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
export CARD="${CARD:?export CARD first}"
mkdir -p "$state_dir"
gates="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.gates.sh"
cscript="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.c.sh"
exclusive="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.exclusive.sh"
marker="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.cgates"
tree_file="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.tree"
rm -f "$marker"
case "${MODE:-normal}" in normal|repair) ;; *) echo "the C-group runner takes MODE=normal or MODE=repair, not $MODE" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
export MODE="${MODE:-normal}"
python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$gates" "$cscript" "$exclusive" <<'EXTRACT'
import json, re, sys
plan, card, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"gates": {"I1056-01": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["all", "第一段："], ["all", "第二段，文档门"], ["all", "先执行前置块，再执行门块；每行是一个独立门。Git 轨必须 PASS。"], ["all", "门块："]]}, "c": ["all", "C 组先执行"], "no_c": []}""")
raw = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
chars = list(raw)
F = chr(96) * 3

def mask(lo, hi):
    for k in range(lo, hi):
        if chars[k] != '\n':
            chars[k] = ' '

for m in re.finditer('(?ms)^' + F + r'[^\n]*\n.*?^' + F + r'[ \t]*$', raw):
    mask(m.start(), m.end())
for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^## (?:评审结论与修订记录|实施证据)[^\n]*$', raw):
    n = re.compile(r'(?m)^## ').search(raw, m.end())
    mask(m.start(), n.start() if n else len(raw))
prose = ''.join(chars)
heading = re.compile(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ')

def die(msg):
    print('C-group extract: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def span(scope):
    if scope == 'all':
        return 0, len(prose)
    m = re.search(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ' + re.escape(card) + r'(?![\w-])', prose)
    if not m:
        die('no Task heading for ' + card)
    n = heading.search(prose, m.end())
    return m.start(), (n.start() if n else len(prose))

def block(scope, anchor):
    lo, hi = span(scope)
    hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(anchor), prose[lo:hi])]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        die(f'anchor {anchor!r} matched {len(hits)} times in the prose of scope {scope}')
    pos = lo + hits[0]
    m = re.compile('(?m)^' + F + r'bash\n').search(raw, pos)
    if not m:
        die(f'no bash block after {anchor!r}')
    if re.search(r'(?m)^#{1,6} ', prose[pos:m.start()]):
        die(f'a heading separates {anchor!r} from its bash block')
    end = raw.find('\n' + F + '\n', m.end() - 1)
    if end < 0:
        die(f'unterminated bash block after {anchor!r}')
    return raw[m.end():end + 1]

if card not in cfg['gates']:
    die('unknown CARD=' + card)
c_out = sys.argv[4]
if c_out != '-' and card in cfg['no_c']:
    die(card + ' has no C-group commit; the C-group runner does not apply')
head = ['set -eo pipefail', 'cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra', 'export CARD=' + card]
parts = head + ['export VSRC=worktree']
for i, (scope, anchor) in enumerate(cfg['gates'][card], 1):
    parts += ['# --- gate block %d: %s' % (i, anchor), 'set +u', block(scope, anchor)]
parts.append('echo "gate blocks passed for $CARD"')
open(out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(parts) + '\n')
if c_out != '-':
    c_parts = head + ['# --- C-group script: %s' % cfg['c'][1], block(cfg['c'][0], cfg['c'][1]), 'echo "C-group script passed for $CARD"']
    open(c_out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(c_parts) + '\n')
if len(sys.argv) > 5:
    open(sys.argv[5], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(block('all', '**工作区独占检查（'))

EXTRACT
MODE=snapshot bash "$exclusive"
bash "$gates"
bash "$cscript"
MODE=verify bash "$exclusive"
printf 'TREE=%s\nGATES=%s\nC=%s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$tree_file" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$(shasum -a 256 "$gates" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$(shasum -a 256 "$cscript" | cut -d' ' -f1)" > "$marker"
cat "$marker"
```

**C 组结论携带检查（`bash` 运行，`CARD` 为本卡 ID；发布四段开头恢复步骤的第 4 小步使用）：** rebase 之后，若上游增量（最初的基线到新的 `origin/main`）与重放前后两次提交之间的差异都只是 `docs/development/plan/` 下、文件名在 `tests/`、`src/`、`build.rs` 与 `.github/` 中都没有出现的计划文件，被测的构建与测试输入就与运行器验证过的树相同，C 组结论随重放携带，不必整套重跑；`plan-20260902.md`、`plan-20260924.md` 等被测试编入，按文件名检索会排除。`plan-status.md` 按行判定：唯一读取它的测试是 `tests/compat/agent_architecture_guard.rs:8181-8215`，只看含 ``[`plan-20260924.md`]``、`DEP-ACF-MIRROR` 或 `DEP-ACF-CAP` 的行（2026-11-07 以 `rg plan-status tests src build.rs .github` 核实只有这一处），所以 `libra diff -U0` 显示的增删行都不含这三个模式时可以携带，含其中任一模式、或 diff 失败都不携带；其它任何增量都必须用 C 组运行器整套重跑。本检查先核对快照与通过标记仍属于最初的 C 组提交（连续多次携带时沿用最初的提交与基线），再在新树上重跑 bump 前窗口检查与版本面内容检查（`VSRC=head`），最后写下携带标记（最初的 C 组提交与基线、新的 `HEAD`、通过标记的 SHA-256）。`PHASE=amend` 的暂存守卫只接受指向当前 `HEAD` 的携带标记，并重新核对同样的条件。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
state_dir=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE_DIR=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE="$STATE_DIR/1056-${CARD:?export CARD first}.env"
state_get() { if [ -f "$STATE" ]; then awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$STATE"; fi; }
state_put() { mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; touch "$STATE"; awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") != 1' "$STATE" > "$STATE.tmp"; printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$STATE.tmp"; mv "$STATE.tmp" "$STATE"; }
export CARD
marker="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.cgates"
tree_file="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.tree"
carry="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.carry"
case "${MODE:-normal}" in normal|repair) ;; *) echo "MODE must be normal or repair, not $MODE" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
test -s "$marker"
test -s "$tree_file"
current_c=$(state_get C_COMMIT)
test -n "$current_c"
orig_c=$current_c
if [ -s "$carry" ]; then
  carry_get() { awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$carry"; }
  prev_to=$(carry_get TO)
  between=$(libra diff --name-only "$prev_to" "$current_c")
  if [ -z "$(printf '%s\n' "$between" | grep -vxF "$plan" | grep . || true)" ]; then orig_c=$(carry_get ORIG_C); fi
fi
orig_base=$(libra rev-parse "$orig_c^")
if [ "$(head -n1 "$tree_file")" != "HEAD $orig_base" ]; then echo "the C-group snapshot does not belong to $orig_c; rerun the C-group runner" >&2; exit 1; fi
head=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
test "$(libra cat-file -p HEAD | grep -c '^parent ')" = 1
new_base=$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)
libra fetch origin
test "$new_base" = "$(libra rev-parse origin/main)"
status_delta_ok() {
  local delta hits
  delta=$(libra diff -U0 "$1" "$2" -- docs/development/plan/plan-status.md | awk '/^(\+\+\+|---) (a|b)\// { next } /^[-+]/ { print }') || return 1
  hits=$(printf '%s\n' "$delta" | grep -cF -e '[`plan-20260924.md`]' -e 'DEP-ACF-MIRROR' -e 'DEP-ACF-CAP' || true)
  [ "$hits" = 0 ]
}
for path in $(libra diff --name-only "$orig_base" "$new_base") $(libra diff --name-only "$orig_c" "$head"); do
  case "$path" in
    docs/development/plan/plan-status.md) ;;
    docs/development/plan/*)
      set +e
      rg -l -F -- "${path##*/}" tests src build.rs .github >/dev/null
      rg_rc=$?
      set -e
      if [ "$rg_rc" != 1 ]; then echo "not carryable: $path is referenced by tests or the build (rg exit $rg_rc); rerun the C-group runner" >&2; exit 1; fi ;;
    *) echo "not carryable: $path; rerun the C-group runner" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
if ! status_delta_ok "$orig_base" "$new_base" || ! status_delta_ok "$orig_c" "$head"; then
  echo "not carryable: a plan-status.md line read by tests/compat/agent_architecture_guard.rs:8181-8215 changed (or the diff failed); rerun the C-group runner" >&2
  exit 1
fi
checks=$(mktemp /Volumes/Data/tmp/carry-checks.XXXXXX)
python3 -I - "$plan" "$checks" <<'CARRYX'
import re, sys
text = open(sys.argv[1], encoding='utf-8').read()
fence = chr(96) * 3
out = ['set -eo pipefail', 'cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra', 'export VSRC=head']
for label in (r'\*\*bump 前窗口检查（`bash` 运行', r'\*\*版本面内容检查（`bash` 运行'):
    hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer('(?m)^' + label, text)]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        sys.exit('label matched %d times' % len(hits))
    m = re.compile('(?m)^' + fence + r'bash\n').search(text, hits[0])
    end = text.find('\n' + fence + '\n', m.end() - 1)
    out.append(text[m.end():end + 1])
open(sys.argv[2], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(out) + '\n')
CARRYX
bash "$checks"
printf 'ORIG_C=%s\nORIG_BASE=%s\nTO=%s\nCGATES=%s\n' "$orig_c" "$orig_base" "$head" "$(shasum -a 256 "$marker" | cut -d' ' -f1)" > "$carry"
cat "$carry"
```

**bump 前窗口检查（`bash` 运行，`MODE` 取 `normal`（缺省）或 `repair`）：** `HEAD` 等于 `origin/main`（bump 前），或是父提交为 `origin/main` 的本卡 C 组提交（rebase 恢复时）。`origin/main` 的四个版本面（`Cargo.toml`、`Cargo.lock` 中 `libra` 包、`install.sh`、`install.ps1`）必须是同一版本，不一致时说明上游的版本面本身有漂移，停止并报告，不在其上 bump。正常模式要求 `origin/main` 的版本已经有 tag，说明没有任何计划（包括 issues/1050–1057 以外同样改版本面的计划）处于「已 bump、未发布」状态；修复模式要求 `origin/main` 的版本就是本卡状态文件里的 `VERSION`、它没有 tag，且上一次的 `PUSHED_SHA` 是 `origin/main` 的祖先。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) allowed='src/command/write_tree.rs tests/command/write_tree_test.rs docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/README.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='240'; c_phase=yes; row_file='docs/development/commands/README.md'; row_prefix='| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
STATE_DIR=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE="$STATE_DIR/1056-${CARD:?export CARD first}.env"
state_get() { if [ -f "$STATE" ]; then awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$STATE"; fi; }
state_put() { mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; touch "$STATE"; awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") != 1' "$STATE" > "$STATE.tmp"; printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$STATE.tmp"; mv "$STATE.tmp" "$STATE"; }
surfaces_of() {
  local toml lock sh ps
  toml=$(libra cat-file -p "$1:Cargo.toml" | awk -F'"' '/^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
  lock=$(libra cat-file -p "$1:Cargo.lock" | awk -F'"' '/^name = "libra"$/ { hit = 1; next } hit && /^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
  sh=$(libra cat-file -p "$1:install.sh" | awk -F'"' '/^DEFAULT_VERSION=/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
  ps=$(libra cat-file -p "$1:install.ps1" | awk -F'"' -v p='$DefaultVersion = ' 'index($0, p) == 1 && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
  printf '%s %s %s %s\n' "$toml" "$lock" "$sh" "$ps"
}
tag_status() {
  local code
  set +e
  code=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/tags/v$1" -i 2>/dev/null | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  set -e
  printf '%s\n' "$code"
}
libra fetch origin
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
head=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
if [ "$head" != "$remote_main" ]; then
  test "$(libra cat-file -p HEAD | grep -c '^parent ')" = 1
  parent=$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)
  test "$parent" = "$remote_main"
fi
read -r current lock_v sh_v ps_v <<< "$(surfaces_of "$remote_main")"
test -n "$current"
if [ "$lock_v" != "$current" ] || [ "$sh_v" != "v$current" ] || [ "$ps_v" != "v$current" ]; then
  echo "origin/main version surfaces disagree: Cargo.toml=$current Cargo.lock=$lock_v install.sh=$sh_v install.ps1=$ps_v" >&2
  exit 1
fi
code=$(tag_status "$current")
case "${MODE:-normal}" in
  normal)
    test "$code" = 200 ;;
  repair)
    test "$code" = 404
    test "$current" = "$(state_get VERSION)"
    previous=$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)
    test -n "$previous"
    rel=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/compare/${previous}...${remote_main}" --jq .status)
    case "$rel" in ahead|identical) ;; *) echo "previous push $previous is not on origin/main ($rel)" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac ;;
  *) echo "unknown MODE=$MODE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
echo "window open for $CARD (${MODE:-normal}, origin/main at v$current)"
```

**版本面内容检查（`bash` 运行，`VSRC=worktree` 检查工作区，`VSRC=head` 检查 `HEAD` 提交；`MODE` 取 `normal`（缺省）或 `repair`）：** 相对 `origin/main`，正常模式下四个版本面文件只允许各改一处版本行，且新版本恰为 `origin/main` 版本的 patch + 1；修复模式下四个文件都不得改动。其它任何行变化都会失败，避免并发会话对这些文件的改动混进发布提交。四个版本面在 `origin/main` 上一致已由窗口检查保证。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${VSRC:?export VSRC=worktree or VSRC=head}" in worktree|head) ;; *) echo "unknown VSRC=$VSRC" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
case "${MODE:-normal}" in normal|repair) ;; *) echo "unknown MODE=$MODE" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
version_of() { libra cat-file -p "$1:Cargo.toml" | awk -F'"' '/^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }'; }
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
base_version=$(version_of "$remote_main")
test -n "$base_version"
new_version=$(printf '%s\n' "$base_version" | awk -F. '{ printf "%d.%d.%d\n", $1, $2, $3 + 1 }')
vtmp=$(mktemp -d /Volumes/Data/tmp/vsurf.XXXXXX)
trap 'rm -rf "$vtmp"' EXIT
for f in Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1; do
  libra cat-file -p "$remote_main:$f" > "$vtmp/base"
  if [ "$VSRC" = head ]; then libra cat-file -p "HEAD:$f" > "$vtmp/new"; else cp "$f" "$vtmp/new"; fi
  changes=$(diff "$vtmp/base" "$vtmp/new" | grep '^[<>]' || true)
  if [ "${MODE:-normal}" = repair ]; then
    want=
  else
    case "$f" in
      Cargo.toml|Cargo.lock) want=$(printf '< version = "%s"\n> version = "%s"' "$base_version" "$new_version") ;;
      install.sh) want=$(printf '< DEFAULT_VERSION="v%s"\n> DEFAULT_VERSION="v%s"' "$base_version" "$new_version") ;;
      install.ps1) want=$(printf '< $DefaultVersion = "v%s"\n> $DefaultVersion = "v%s"' "$base_version" "$new_version") ;;
    esac
  fi
  if [ "$changes" != "$want" ]; then echo "unexpected version-surface change in $f" >&2; exit 1; fi
done
echo "version surfaces OK (${MODE:-normal}): base $base_version"
```

**ER-05 审查摘要（`bash` 运行，`CARD` 为本卡 ID；有 C 组提交的卡在 ER-05 PASS 后运行，输出写成 `<CARD>:ER05_REVIEWED=` 的值）：** 对工作区里本卡的产品改动取摘要：逐个列出未提交与未跟踪的路径（`--untracked-files=all`，目录展开到文件），去掉本文件、`plan-status.md` 与四个版本面文件，按路径排序后写成「内容 SHA-256 与路径」一行一个（已删除的写 `deleted`），再取整体 SHA-256。没有产品改动的卡（如只做版本承载的发布卡）得到空集合的摘要 `e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855`。暂存守卫 `PHASE=c` 内含同一函数。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
review_digest() {
  libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort | while IFS= read -r path; do
    case "$path" in "$plan"|"$status_file"|Cargo.toml|Cargo.lock|install.sh|install.ps1) continue ;; esac
    if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
  done | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1
}
review_digest
```

**精确句预检（`bash` 运行，有网站面的卡在提交前执行）：** 三份仓库文档各自至少有一行恰好包含本卡精确句，且这一行在围栏代码块之外（代码块经语法高亮后会被拆成多个 `span`，网站段三按原始 HTML 匹配的检查就找不到它）；句子不能折行，也不能加反引号或其它行内标记。本卡承诺在 `COMPATIBILITY.md` 某行内追加精确句的（`case` 里的 `compat_sent`），该行也必须含精确句。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) CMDS='write-tree'; sentence='With a prefix, write-tree validates the whole index and writes all of its trees, then prints the object ID of the subtree at that directory; a missing prefix fails with exit code 128.'; compat_sent='' ;;
  *) echo "no website sentence for CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
for command in $CMDS; do
  for doc in "docs/commands/${command}.md" "docs/commands/zh-CN/${command}.md" "docs/development/commands/${command}.md"; do
    outside=$(awk -v s="$sentence" 'index($0, "\140\140\140") == 1 { fence = !fence; next } !fence && index($0, s) { n++ } END { print n + 0 }' "$doc")
    test "$outside" -ge 1
  done
done
for n in $compat_sent; do
  test "$(sed -n "${n}p" COMPATIBILITY.md | grep -cF "$sentence")" -ge 1
done
```

**暂存守卫（`bash` 运行，`PHASE` 取 `start`、`accept`、`c`、`amend`、`remote`、`terminal`、`blocked` 或 `abandon`）：** 暂存区开始时必须为空；工作区里的改动路径都必须在该 `PHASE` 允许留在工作区的集合内；`PHASE=c` 与 `PHASE=amend` 时再核对 C 组快照未变，并要求 C 组通过标记与从本文件重新抽出的脚本一致；`PHASE=c` 另要求「实施证据」有本卡的四行 ER-05 收据（每个键恰好一行，`ER05_VERDICT=PASS`），且 `ER05_REVIEWED` 等于在当前树上重算的 ER-05 审查摘要；`amend` 时要么运行器已在重放后的树上重跑过（快照与通过标记都对应当前树），要么有 C 组结论携带检查写下的携带标记（见该检查：上游增量只含不被测试与构建读取的计划文件，标记指向当前 `HEAD`、绑定当前通过标记），守卫会重新核对携带条件；卡字段的转移按 `PHASE` 核对（读 `HEAD` 与工作区的本文件比较）：`start` 只能 `pending` / 空 → `in-progress` / 空，`accept` 只能 `in-progress` / 空 → `in-progress` / `locally-accepted`，`c` 只能写 `locally-accepted` 或保持 `locally-accepted`、`remote-pending`（修复与前滚），`amend` 不改，`remote` 只能 → `remote-pending`（修复后的再次登记保持不变），`terminal` 只能 `in-progress` / `remote-pending` → `done` / `complete`，`blocked` 只能在 `in-progress` 与 `blocked` 之间切换且 `Acceptance` 等于 `HEAD` 已提交的值（工作区里未提交的 `locally-accepted` 要先改回），`abandon` 只能从 `in-progress` 或 `blocked`（`Acceptance` 为空或 `locally-accepted`）回到 `pending` / 空，且状态文件没有 `PUSHED_SHA`；同一提交里其它卡的字段都不得改变（本计划登记的联动卡在 `remote`、`terminal` 时与本卡同步转移）。每个 `PHASE` 都要求 `plan-status.md` 里本计划行在工作区与 `HEAD` 各恰好一行，并且要暂存的路径里没有冲突标记行（`<<<<<<< `、`||||||| `、`>>>>>>> ` 开头或恰为 `=======` 的行）；`abandon` 另向 GitHub 核对第 9 步列出的「没有推送过」条件。`remote` 还要求「实施证据」有本卡的 `<CARD>:C_COMMIT=`、`<CARD>:PUSHED_SHA=`、`<CARD>:VERSION=` 行且与状态文件一致，并向 GitHub 确认该 SHA 在远端 `main` 上、候选分支指向它；`terminal` 要求本卡的 `CI_BASE_RUN`、`CI_CODEQL_RUN`、`TAG`、`RELEASE_RUN`、`CANDIDATE_DELETED`、`ROLLBACK_VERIFIED`（有网站面时另加 `SITE_SHA`、`SITE_VERIFIED` 与 `SITE_REVIEWED`，并要求 `SITE_REVIEW_SESSION`、`SITE_REVIEW_OUTPUT_SHA256`、`SITE_REVIEW_VERDICT=PASS` 与 `SITE_REVIEWED` 各恰好一行）各行与状态文件一致，并向 GitHub 重新核对：tag 指向 `PUSHED_SHA`，两个候选分支 run 与 release run 都是 success 且 head SHA 是 `PUSHED_SHA`，五个 release job 全部 success，候选分支已删除；有网站面的卡还要求 `<CARD>:SITE_VERIFIED=` 行（网站段三确认两个线上页面含精确句后写入），并重新取回 EN 与 zh-CN 两个线上页面确认含精确句、后端 `origin/cf` 包含 `SITE_SHA`；`accept` 从本文件抽出本卡的门块重跑一遍；`COMPATIBILITY.md` 的 hunk：本卡第一次 C 组提交（状态文件还没有 `PUSHED_SHA`）必须改登记的行，之后的修复或前滚提交可以不改，改了仍按下述单行替换核对；`plan-status.md` 的未暂存差异按 hunk 解析，每个增删行（含空行与以 `-`/`+` 开头的内容行）都必须是本计划行；`COMPATIBILITY.md` 的每个 hunk 只能是本卡登记行号上的单行替换（`-n,1 +n,1`），且该行在 `HEAD` 与工作区都是预期的命令行；共享索引文件（如有登记）的差异行都必须以本卡的行前缀开头且增删行数相等；暂存后已暂存路径必须全部在该 `PHASE` 的暂存集合内。`start`、`remote`、`terminal` 只允许并暂存本文件与 `plan-status.md`；`accept` 同样只允许这两个文件，且只用于没有 C 组提交的卡；`blocked` 只暂存这两个文件，但允许本卡写集内未提交的改动留在工作区；`abandon` 只允许并暂存这两个文件（本卡的其它改动必须先撤销）；`amend` 只允许本文件（rebase 恢复后更新 C 组收据，之后执行 `libra commit --amend -S --no-edit`：重放保留了原提交信息里的 `Signed-off-by`，再加 `-s` 会写出第二行，2026-11-07 已实测；`plan-status.md` 会被测试读取，C 组之后不再改动）。版本面文件的内容由版本面内容检查约束。任一检查失败即停止，不提交。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
state_dir=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) allowed='src/command/write_tree.rs tests/command/write_tree_test.rs docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/README.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='240'; c_phase=yes; row_file='docs/development/commands/README.md'; row_prefix='| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
compat_prefix() {
  case "$1" in
    240) printf '%s\n' '| write-tree | partial | Writes the index out as a nested tree object' ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}
check_compat_rows() {
  local file="$1" n prefix hits
  for n in $compat_lines; do
    prefix=$(compat_prefix "$n")
    hits=$(grep -nF -- "$prefix" "$file" | cut -d: -f1 | tr '\n' ' ')
    if [ "$hits" != "$n " ]; then echo "COMPATIBILITY.md row $n is not the expected row in $file (prefix found at: $hits)" >&2; return 1; fi
  done
}
STATE_DIR=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE="$STATE_DIR/1056-${CARD:?export CARD first}.env"
state_get() { if [ -f "$STATE" ]; then awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$STATE"; fi; }
state_put() { mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; touch "$STATE"; awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") != 1' "$STATE" > "$STATE.tmp"; printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$STATE.tmp"; mv "$STATE.tmp" "$STATE"; }
on_remote_main() {
  local resp code rel
  set +e
  resp=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/compare/$1...main" -i 2>/dev/null)
  set -e
  code=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  case "$code" in
    200) rel=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | sed -n '/^{/,$p' | jq -er .status) || return 1 ;;
    404) printf '%s\n' no; return 0 ;;
    *) echo "cannot compare $1 with remote main (HTTP ${code:-none})" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
  case "$rel" in ahead|identical) printf '%s\n' yes ;; *) printf '%s\n' no ;; esac
}
remote_branch() {
  local resp code
  set +e
  resp=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/heads/$1" -i 2>/dev/null)
  set -e
  code=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  case "$code" in
    200) printf '%s\n' "$resp" | sed -n '/^{/,$p' | jq -er '.object.sha' ;;
    404) printf '%s\n' none ;;
    *) echo "cannot read remote branch $1 (HTTP ${code:-none})" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
}
case "$CARD" in
  I1056-01) site_cmds='write-tree'; site_sentence='With a prefix, write-tree validates the whole index and writes all of its trees, then prints the object ID of the subtree at that directory; a missing prefix fails with exit code 128.' ;;
  *) site_cmds=; site_sentence= ;;
esac
full="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1 $plan $status_file"
check_c=no
case "${PHASE:?export PHASE=start, accept, c, amend, remote, terminal, blocked or abandon}" in
  c)
    if [ "$c_phase" != yes ]; then echo "$CARD has no C-group commit" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$full"; worktree_ok="$full"; check_c=yes ;;
  amend)
    if [ "$c_phase" != yes ]; then echo "$CARD has no C-group commit" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$plan"; worktree_ok="$plan"; compat_lines=; row_file=; check_c=yes ;;
  accept)
    if [ "$c_phase" != no ]; then echo "$CARD records acceptance with its C-group commit, not PHASE=accept" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$stage"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  start|remote|terminal) stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$stage"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  blocked) stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$full"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  abandon)
    if [ -n "$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)" ]; then echo "$CARD has a pushed C-group commit and cannot be abandoned; repair or roll forward instead" >&2; exit 1; fi
    libra fetch origin
    base_version=$(libra cat-file -p HEAD:Cargo.toml | awk -F'"' '/^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
    remote_version=$(libra cat-file -p origin/main:Cargo.toml | awk -F'"' '/^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
    test -n "$base_version"
    if [ "$remote_version" != "$base_version" ]; then echo "origin/main is at $remote_version, not the pre-bump $base_version: a bump may already be on main; do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    recorded_c=$(state_get C_COMMIT)
    if [ -n "$recorded_c" ] && [ "$(on_remote_main "$recorded_c")" != no ]; then echo "C_COMMIT $recorded_c is on remote main (or the check failed); do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    next_version=$(printf '%s\n' "$base_version" | awk -F. '{ printf "%d.%d.%d\n", $1, $2, $3 + 1 }')
    if [ "$(remote_branch "release/v$next_version")" != none ]; then echo "release/v$next_version exists on GitHub; do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    set +e
    tag_code=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/tags/v$next_version" -i 2>/dev/null | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
    set -e
    if [ "$tag_code" != 404 ]; then echo "tag v$next_version exists or could not be checked (HTTP ${tag_code:-none}); do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$stage"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  *) echo "unknown PHASE=$PHASE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
staged_paths() {
  libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { x = substr($0, 1, 1); if (x != " " && x != "?") print substr($0, 4) }'
}
before=$(staged_paths)
test -z "$before"
for path in $(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }'); do
  case " $worktree_ok " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "change outside the allowed set for PHASE=$PHASE: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
if [ "$check_c" = yes ]; then
  tree_file="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.tree"
  marker="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.cgates"
  test -s "$tree_file"
  test -s "$marker"
  printf 'HEAD %s\n' "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)" > "$tree_file.now"
  for path in $(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort); do
    if [ "$path" = "$plan" ]; then continue; fi
    if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
  done >> "$tree_file.now"
  if ! cmp -s "$tree_file" "$tree_file.now"; then
    carry="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.carry"
    if [ "$PHASE" != amend ] || [ ! -s "$carry" ]; then echo "worktree changed since the C-group snapshot" >&2; exit 1; fi
    carry_get() { awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$carry"; }
    test "$(carry_get TO)" = "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)"
    test "$(carry_get CGATES)" = "$(shasum -a 256 "$marker" | cut -d' ' -f1)"
    orig_c=$(carry_get ORIG_C)
    orig_base=$(carry_get ORIG_BASE)
    test "$(head -n1 "$tree_file")" = "HEAD $orig_base"
    test "$(libra rev-parse "$orig_c^")" = "$orig_base"
    status_delta_ok() {
      local delta hits
      delta=$(libra diff -U0 "$1" "$2" -- docs/development/plan/plan-status.md | awk '/^(\+\+\+|---) (a|b)\// { next } /^[-+]/ { print }') || return 1
      hits=$(printf '%s\n' "$delta" | grep -cF -e '[`plan-20260924.md`]' -e 'DEP-ACF-MIRROR' -e 'DEP-ACF-CAP' || true)
      [ "$hits" = 0 ]
    }
    for path in $(libra diff --name-only "$orig_base" "$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)") $(libra diff --name-only "$orig_c" HEAD); do
      case "$path" in
        docs/development/plan/plan-status.md) ;;
        docs/development/plan/*)
          set +e
          rg -l -F -- "${path##*/}" tests src build.rs .github >/dev/null
          rg_rc=$?
          set -e
          if [ "$rg_rc" != 1 ]; then echo "not carryable: $path is referenced by tests or the build (rg exit $rg_rc)" >&2; exit 1; fi ;;
        *) echo "not carryable: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
      esac
    done
    if ! status_delta_ok "$orig_base" "$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)" || ! status_delta_ok "$orig_c" "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)"; then
      echo "not carryable: a plan-status.md line read by tests changed (or the diff failed)" >&2
      exit 1
    fi
  fi
  fresh="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.gates.check.sh"
  fresh_c="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.c.check.sh"
  python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$fresh" "$fresh_c" <<'EXTRACT'
import json, re, sys
plan, card, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"gates": {"I1056-01": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["all", "第一段："], ["all", "第二段，文档门"], ["all", "先执行前置块，再执行门块；每行是一个独立门。Git 轨必须 PASS。"], ["all", "门块："]]}, "c": ["all", "C 组先执行"], "no_c": []}""")
raw = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
chars = list(raw)
F = chr(96) * 3

def mask(lo, hi):
    for k in range(lo, hi):
        if chars[k] != '\n':
            chars[k] = ' '

for m in re.finditer('(?ms)^' + F + r'[^\n]*\n.*?^' + F + r'[ \t]*$', raw):
    mask(m.start(), m.end())
for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^## (?:评审结论与修订记录|实施证据)[^\n]*$', raw):
    n = re.compile(r'(?m)^## ').search(raw, m.end())
    mask(m.start(), n.start() if n else len(raw))
prose = ''.join(chars)
heading = re.compile(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ')

def die(msg):
    print('C-group extract: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def span(scope):
    if scope == 'all':
        return 0, len(prose)
    m = re.search(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ' + re.escape(card) + r'(?![\w-])', prose)
    if not m:
        die('no Task heading for ' + card)
    n = heading.search(prose, m.end())
    return m.start(), (n.start() if n else len(prose))

def block(scope, anchor):
    lo, hi = span(scope)
    hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(anchor), prose[lo:hi])]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        die(f'anchor {anchor!r} matched {len(hits)} times in the prose of scope {scope}')
    pos = lo + hits[0]
    m = re.compile('(?m)^' + F + r'bash\n').search(raw, pos)
    if not m:
        die(f'no bash block after {anchor!r}')
    if re.search(r'(?m)^#{1,6} ', prose[pos:m.start()]):
        die(f'a heading separates {anchor!r} from its bash block')
    end = raw.find('\n' + F + '\n', m.end() - 1)
    if end < 0:
        die(f'unterminated bash block after {anchor!r}')
    return raw[m.end():end + 1]

if card not in cfg['gates']:
    die('unknown CARD=' + card)
c_out = sys.argv[4]
if c_out != '-' and card in cfg['no_c']:
    die(card + ' has no C-group commit; the C-group runner does not apply')
head = ['set -eo pipefail', 'cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra', 'export CARD=' + card]
parts = head + ['export VSRC=worktree']
for i, (scope, anchor) in enumerate(cfg['gates'][card], 1):
    parts += ['# --- gate block %d: %s' % (i, anchor), 'set +u', block(scope, anchor)]
parts.append('echo "gate blocks passed for $CARD"')
open(out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(parts) + '\n')
if c_out != '-':
    c_parts = head + ['# --- C-group script: %s' % cfg['c'][1], block(cfg['c'][0], cfg['c'][1]), 'echo "C-group script passed for $CARD"']
    open(c_out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(c_parts) + '\n')
if len(sys.argv) > 5:
    open(sys.argv[5], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(block('all', '**工作区独占检查（'))

EXTRACT
  want=$(printf 'TREE=%s\nGATES=%s\nC=%s' "$(shasum -a 256 "$tree_file" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$(shasum -a 256 "$fresh" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$(shasum -a 256 "$fresh_c" | cut -d' ' -f1)")
  if [ "$(cat "$marker")" != "$want" ]; then echo "no C-group pass marker for this tree and these gate blocks; rerun the C-group runner" >&2; exit 1; fi
fi
if [ "$PHASE" = c ]; then
  review_digest() {
    libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort | while IFS= read -r path; do
      case "$path" in "$plan"|"$status_file"|Cargo.toml|Cargo.lock|install.sh|install.ps1) continue ;; esac
      if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
    done | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1
  }
  er05_line() {
    local hits
    hits=$(grep -E "^$CARD:$1=$2\$" "$plan" || true)
    if [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$hits" | grep -c .)" != 1 ]; then echo "need exactly one line $CARD:$1=<$2> in the implementation evidence" >&2; return 1; fi
    printf '%s\n' "${hits#*=}"
  }
  er05_line ER05_SESSION '[0-9A-Za-z._:-]{8,}' >/dev/null
  er05_line ER05_OUTPUT_SHA256 '[0-9a-f]{64}' >/dev/null
  er05_line ER05_VERDICT 'PASS' >/dev/null
  reviewed=$(er05_line ER05_REVIEWED '[0-9a-f]{64}')
  current=$(review_digest)
  if [ "$reviewed" != "$current" ]; then echo "product files changed after the ER-05 review (reviewed $reviewed, now $current); review again and replace the four ER-05 lines" >&2; exit 1; fi
fi
own='| [`issues/1056.md`](issues/1056.md) |'
if [ "$(grep -cF -- "$own" "$status_file")" != 1 ]; then echo "plan-status.md must contain exactly one row for this plan in the worktree" >&2; exit 1; fi
if [ "$(libra cat-file -p "HEAD:$status_file" | grep -cF -- "$own")" != 1 ]; then echo "plan-status.md must contain exactly one row for this plan at HEAD" >&2; exit 1; fi
for path in $stage; do
  if [ -f "$path" ] && grep -nE '^(<<<<<<< |\|\|\|\|\|\|\| |>>>>>>> |=======$)' "$path" >&2; then echo "conflict marker lines in $path" >&2; exit 1; fi
done
status_diff=$(libra diff -U0 "$status_file")
foreign=$(printf '%s\n' "$status_diff" | awk -v p="$own" '
  /^@@/ { in_hunk = 1; next }
  !in_hunk { next }
  /^[-+]/ { body = substr($0, 2); if (index(body, p) != 1) print "foreign line: " $0 }
')
test -z "$foreign"
if [ -n "$compat_lines" ]; then
  check_compat_rows COMPATIBILITY.md
  libra cat-file -p HEAD:COMPATIBILITY.md > "$state_dir/1056-$CARD.compat-head"
  check_compat_rows "$state_dir/1056-$CARD.compat-head"
  compat_diff=$(libra diff -U0 COMPATIBILITY.md)
  if [ -z "$compat_diff" ]; then
    if [ -z "$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)" ]; then echo "the first C-group commit of $CARD must change COMPATIBILITY.md line(s) $compat_lines" >&2; exit 1; fi
  else
    bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$compat_diff" | awk -v ok=" $compat_lines " '
      /^@@/ {
        split($2, o, ","); split($3, n, ",")
        start = substr(o[1], 2); oc = (o[2] == "" ? 1 : o[2]); nc = (n[2] == "" ? 1 : n[2])
        if (oc != 1 || nc != 1 || substr(n[1], 2) != start || index(ok, " " start " ") == 0) print "unexpected hunk: " $0
      }')
    if [ -n "$bad" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$bad" >&2; exit 1; fi
  fi
fi
if [ -n "$row_file" ]; then
  row_diff=$(libra diff -U0 "$row_file")
  if [ -n "$row_diff" ]; then
    row_bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$row_diff" | awk -v p="$row_prefix" '
      /^@@/ { in_hunk = 1; next }
      !in_hunk { next }
      /^-/ { del++; if (index(substr($0, 2), p) != 1) print "foreign line: " $0 }
      /^\+/ { add++; if (index(substr($0, 2), p) != 1) print "foreign line: " $0 }
      END { if (add != del) print "row count changed: +" add " -" del }')
    test -z "$row_bad"
  fi
fi
python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$PHASE" "$state_dir/1056-$CARD.env" <<'TRANSITION'
import json, re, subprocess, sys
plan, card, phase, state_path = sys.argv[1:5]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"linked": {}, "site": true}""")

def die(msg):
    print('card-state check: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def norm(value):
    value = re.sub('（[^）]*）|[(][^)]*[)]', '', value.replace(chr(96), ''))
    life, _, acc = value.partition('/')
    life = re.split('[（(]', (life.split() or [''])[0])[0]
    acc = re.split('[（(]', (acc.split() or [''])[0])[0].strip('。；;,，')
    return (life, acc if acc not in ('', '空') else '空')

def fields(text):
    out = {}
    for body in re.split(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ', text)[1:]:
        cid = re.split(r'[:：\s]', body, maxsplit=1)[0]
        m = re.search(r'(?m)^\*\*Lifecycle / Acceptance:\*\*[ \t]*(.*)$', body)
        out[cid] = norm(m.group(1)) if m else None
    return out

head = subprocess.run(['libra', 'cat-file', '-p', 'HEAD:' + plan], capture_output=True, text=True)
if head.returncode:
    die('cannot read HEAD:' + plan)
text = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
old, new = fields(head.stdout), fields(text)
if set(old) != set(new):
    die('the set of Task cards differs between HEAD and the worktree')
if new.get(card) is None:
    die('no Lifecycle / Acceptance field for ' + card)
P, LA, RP = ('in-progress', '空'), ('in-progress', 'locally-accepted'), ('in-progress', 'remote-pending')
ACC = ('空', 'locally-accepted', 'remote-pending')
ALLOWED = {
    'start': {(('pending', '空'), P)},
    'accept': {(P, LA)},
    'c': {(P, LA), (LA, LA), (RP, RP)},
    'amend': {(LA, LA), (RP, RP)},
    'remote': {(LA, RP), (RP, RP)},
    'terminal': {(RP, ('done', 'complete'))},
    'blocked': {(('in-progress', a), ('blocked', a)) for a in ACC} | {(('blocked', a), ('in-progress', a)) for a in ACC},
    'abandon': {((life, a), ('pending', '空')) for life in ('in-progress', 'blocked') for a in ('空', 'locally-accepted')},
}
move = (old[card], new[card])
if phase == 'blocked' and old[card][1] != new[card][1]:
    die(f'{card}: PHASE=blocked keeps the committed Acceptance {old[card][1]!r}; put it back in the worktree (an uncommitted locally-accepted is rewritten after unblocking), then rerun')
if move not in ALLOWED[phase]:
    die(f'{card}: {move[0]} -> {move[1]} is not allowed for PHASE={phase}')
linked = cfg['linked'].get(card, [])
for cid in new:
    if cid == card:
        continue
    if cid in linked and phase in ('remote', 'terminal'):
        if (old[cid], new[cid]) != move:
            die(f'{cid} must move with {card}: {old[cid]} -> {new[cid]}')
    elif old[cid] != new[cid]:
        die(f'{cid} changed ({old[cid]} -> {new[cid]}) in a PHASE={phase} commit for {card}')
state = {}
try:
    with open(state_path, encoding='utf-8') as fh:
        for line in fh:
            key, sep, value = line.rstrip('\n').partition('=')
            if sep:
                state[key] = value
except FileNotFoundError:
    pass
keys = {'remote': ['C_COMMIT', 'PUSHED_SHA', 'VERSION'],
        'terminal': ['CI_BASE_RUN', 'CI_CODEQL_RUN', 'TAG', 'RELEASE_RUN', 'CANDIDATE_DELETED', 'ROLLBACK_VERIFIED'] + (['SITE_SHA', 'SITE_VERIFIED', 'SITE_REVIEWED'] if cfg['site'] else [])}.get(phase, [])
for key in keys:
    value = state.get(key, '')
    if not value:
        die(f'the state file has no {key} for {card}')
    if not re.search('(?m)^' + re.escape(f'{card}:{key}={value}') + '$', text):
        die(f'the implementation evidence needs the line {card}:{key}={value}')
if phase == 'terminal' and state.get('CANDIDATE_DELETED') != 'yes':
    die('CANDIDATE_DELETED is not yes')
if phase == 'terminal' and cfg['site']:
    for key, pat in (('SITE_REVIEW_SESSION', '[0-9A-Za-z._:-]{8,}'), ('SITE_REVIEW_OUTPUT_SHA256', '[0-9a-f]{64}'), ('SITE_REVIEW_VERDICT', 'PASS'), ('SITE_REVIEWED', '[0-9a-f]{64}')):
        if len(re.findall('(?m)^' + re.escape(card + ':' + key + '=') + pat + '$', text)) != 1:
            die(f'the implementation evidence needs exactly one line {card}:{key}=<{pat}>')

TRANSITION
case "$PHASE" in
  remote)
    pushed=$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)
    test "$(on_remote_main "$pushed")" = yes
    test "$(remote_branch "release/v$(state_get VERSION)")" = "$pushed" ;;
  terminal)
    pushed=$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)
    version=$(state_get VERSION)
    test -n "$pushed"
    test -n "$version"
    test "$(state_get TAG)" = "v$version"
    ref=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/tags/v$version" --jq '"\(.object.type) \(.object.sha)"')
    read -r ttype tobj <<< "$ref"
    if [ "$ttype" = tag ]; then tobj=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/tags/$tobj" --jq .object.sha); fi
    test "$tobj" = "$pushed"
    for key in BASE CODEQL; do
      run=$(state_get "CI_${key}_RUN")
      test -n "$run"
      view=$(gh run view "$run" --repo libra-tools/libra --json conclusion,headSha,headBranch,event --jq '"\(.conclusion) \(.headSha) \(.headBranch) \(.event)"')
      test "$view" = "success $pushed release/v$version push"
    done
    release_run=$(state_get RELEASE_RUN)
    test -n "$release_run"
    rview=$(gh run view "$release_run" --repo libra-tools/libra --json conclusion,headSha,headBranch --jq '"\(.conclusion) \(.headSha) \(.headBranch)"')
    test "$rview" = "success $pushed v$version"
    gh run view "$release_run" --repo libra-tools/libra --json jobs |
      jq -e '.jobs as $jobs
        | ["build-and-upload","upload-install-scripts","update-homebrew-tap","verify-homebrew-formula","request-stable-manifest"]
        | all(.[]; . as $j | [$jobs[] | select(.name | startswith($j))] | (length > 0) and all(.[]; .conclusion == "success"))' >/dev/null
    test "$(remote_branch "release/v$version")" = none
    if [ -n "$site_cmds" ]; then
      site_sha=$(state_get SITE_SHA)
      test -n "$site_sha"
      test -n "$(state_get SITE_VERIFIED)"
      for command in $site_cmds; do
        for locale in en zh-CN; do
          body=$(curl -fsS --max-time 60 "https://libra.tools/${locale}/docs/commands/${command}")
          test "$(printf '%s\n' "$body" | grep -cF "$site_sentence")" -ge 1
        done
      done
      (
        cd /Volumes/Sea/libra-backend
        test "$(pwd -P)" = /Volumes/Sea/libra-backend
        libra fetch origin
        test "$(libra rev-list origin/cf | grep -cxF "$site_sha")" = 1
      )
    fi ;;
  accept)
    accept_gates="$state_dir/1056-$CARD.accept.sh"
    python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$accept_gates" - <<'EXTRACT'
import json, re, sys
plan, card, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"gates": {"I1056-01": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["all", "第一段："], ["all", "第二段，文档门"], ["all", "先执行前置块，再执行门块；每行是一个独立门。Git 轨必须 PASS。"], ["all", "门块："]]}, "c": ["all", "C 组先执行"], "no_c": []}""")
raw = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
chars = list(raw)
F = chr(96) * 3

def mask(lo, hi):
    for k in range(lo, hi):
        if chars[k] != '\n':
            chars[k] = ' '

for m in re.finditer('(?ms)^' + F + r'[^\n]*\n.*?^' + F + r'[ \t]*$', raw):
    mask(m.start(), m.end())
for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^## (?:评审结论与修订记录|实施证据)[^\n]*$', raw):
    n = re.compile(r'(?m)^## ').search(raw, m.end())
    mask(m.start(), n.start() if n else len(raw))
prose = ''.join(chars)
heading = re.compile(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ')

def die(msg):
    print('C-group extract: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def span(scope):
    if scope == 'all':
        return 0, len(prose)
    m = re.search(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ' + re.escape(card) + r'(?![\w-])', prose)
    if not m:
        die('no Task heading for ' + card)
    n = heading.search(prose, m.end())
    return m.start(), (n.start() if n else len(prose))

def block(scope, anchor):
    lo, hi = span(scope)
    hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(anchor), prose[lo:hi])]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        die(f'anchor {anchor!r} matched {len(hits)} times in the prose of scope {scope}')
    pos = lo + hits[0]
    m = re.compile('(?m)^' + F + r'bash\n').search(raw, pos)
    if not m:
        die(f'no bash block after {anchor!r}')
    if re.search(r'(?m)^#{1,6} ', prose[pos:m.start()]):
        die(f'a heading separates {anchor!r} from its bash block')
    end = raw.find('\n' + F + '\n', m.end() - 1)
    if end < 0:
        die(f'unterminated bash block after {anchor!r}')
    return raw[m.end():end + 1]

if card not in cfg['gates']:
    die('unknown CARD=' + card)
c_out = sys.argv[4]
if c_out != '-' and card in cfg['no_c']:
    die(card + ' has no C-group commit; the C-group runner does not apply')
head = ['set -eo pipefail', 'cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra', 'export CARD=' + card]
parts = head + ['export VSRC=worktree']
for i, (scope, anchor) in enumerate(cfg['gates'][card], 1):
    parts += ['# --- gate block %d: %s' % (i, anchor), 'set +u', block(scope, anchor)]
parts.append('echo "gate blocks passed for $CARD"')
open(out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(parts) + '\n')
if c_out != '-':
    c_parts = head + ['# --- C-group script: %s' % cfg['c'][1], block(cfg['c'][0], cfg['c'][1]), 'echo "C-group script passed for $CARD"']
    open(c_out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(c_parts) + '\n')
if len(sys.argv) > 5:
    open(sys.argv[5], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(block('all', '**工作区独占检查（'))

EXTRACT
    bash "$accept_gates" ;;
esac
for path in $stage; do
  if [ -e "$path" ]; then libra add "$path"; fi
done
staged=$(staged_paths)
test -n "$staged"
for path in $staged; do
  case " $stage " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "staged path outside the PHASE=$PHASE set: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
```

**提交后范围检查（`bash` 运行，`PHASE` 取 `start`、`accept`、`c`、`remote`、`terminal`、`blocked` 或 `abandon`；amend 之后用 `c`）：** 刚生成的提交只能改该 `PHASE` 允许集合内的路径，且只有一个父提交。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) allowed='src/command/write_tree.rs tests/command/write_tree_test.rs docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/README.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='240'; c_phase=yes; row_file='docs/development/commands/README.md'; row_prefix='| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
case "${PHASE:?export PHASE=start, accept, c, remote, terminal, blocked or abandon}" in
  c)
    if [ "$c_phase" != yes ]; then echo "$CARD has no C-group commit" >&2; exit 1; fi
    allowed="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1 $plan $status_file" ;;
  accept)
    if [ "$c_phase" != no ]; then echo "$CARD records acceptance with its C-group commit, not PHASE=accept" >&2; exit 1; fi
    allowed="$plan $status_file" ;;
  start|remote|terminal|blocked|abandon) allowed="$plan $status_file" ;;
  *) echo "unknown PHASE=$PHASE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
test "$(libra cat-file -p HEAD | grep -c '^parent ')" = 1
changed=$(libra diff --name-only HEAD^ HEAD)
test -n "$changed"
for path in $changed; do
  case " $allowed " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "committed path outside write set: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
test "$(libra cat-file -p "HEAD:$status_file" | grep -cF -- '| [`issues/1056.md`](issues/1056.md) |')" = 1
```

**计划目录推送脚本（`bash` 运行，`PHASE` 取 `start`、`accept`、`remote`、`terminal`、`blocked` 或 `abandon`）：** 推送前核对：暂存区为空；远端 `main` 上没有其它提交的在途 run（只放行提交说明以 `(#1056)` 结尾的本计划提交的 run，`gh` 查询或读取提交失败同样停止；推送会让 `cancel-superseded-main` 取消它们，所以有就停止，等它们结束后原样重跑）；工作区干净（`PHASE=blocked` 时允许本卡允许集合内未提交的改动）；当前分支是 `main` 且指向被检查的提交；该提交只有一个父提交，带 `gpgsig` 与 `Signed-off-by`（直接读 `libra cat-file -p` 的提交对象：`libra log -1 --only-trailers --trailer Signed-off-by` 会返回最近一个带该 trailer 的祖先提交，2026-11-07 已实测，不能用来证明本提交）；它只改本文件与 `plan-status.md`。`PHASE=start` 时，在 fetch 并确认父提交就是 `origin/main` 之后、推送之前，从本文件抽出开工门原样重跑。被检查提交的父提交必须已在远端 `main` 上：父提交是尚未推送的提交（例如 C 组提交）时脚本直接拒绝，不进入 rebase，因为推送会把那个提交一并带上 `main`。推送通过一个临时本地分支 `plan-push-1056-<CARD>` 进行，该分支在检查之后被强制指向被检查的 SHA，所以推上远端 `main` 的对象就是它，即使本地 `main` 在此期间被移动。推送结果按远端事实判定：fetch 后 `origin/main` 等于该 SHA，或 GitHub compare 显示该 SHA 已在远端 `main` 上，就算推送成功（客户端报错但推送已落地、或他人紧接着推送，都不误判，重跑本脚本也直接确认成功）；`origin/main` 仍是父提交，说明是认证、网络或服务端错误，按 ER-09 直接非零退出，不 rebase、不 amend、不计数；`origin/main` 已前进到别处时进入有界恢复：`libra rebase origin/main`（`PHASE=blocked` 且工作区有本卡未提交改动时加 `--autostash`，rebase 前后由 libra 暂存并恢复这些改动；失败时只有确实存在进行中的 rebase 才 `--abort`，并区分「冲突」与「拒绝开始」），成功后才把计数加一，`libra commit --amend -S --no-edit` 重签（不加 `-s`，原 `Signed-off-by` 已随重放保留），以退出码 3 停止。执行者随后重跑提交后范围检查（`PHASE=start` 时还要重跑 `WHEN=start` 的外部前置检查；开工门由本脚本自己重跑），再运行本脚本。计数文件按计划、卡与 `PHASE` 命名，第 4 次遇到上游前进时脚本非零退出：开工登记未推送时本卡保持 `pending`；其它登记未推送时本卡保持当前状态；都向用户报告，用户决定继续时由执行者删除该计数文件。不用 `libra pull` 合并上游，因为合并提交有两个父提交，推送检查会拒绝。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1056.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1056-01) allowed='src/command/write_tree.rs tests/command/write_tree_test.rs docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/README.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='240'; c_phase=yes; row_file='docs/development/commands/README.md'; row_prefix='| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
on_remote_main() {
  local resp code rel
  set +e
  resp=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/compare/$1...main" -i 2>/dev/null)
  set -e
  code=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  case "$code" in
    200) rel=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | sed -n '/^{/,$p' | jq -er .status) || return 1 ;;
    404) printf '%s\n' no; return 0 ;;
    *) echo "cannot compare $1 with remote main (HTTP ${code:-none})" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
  case "$rel" in ahead|identical) printf '%s\n' yes ;; *) printf '%s\n' no ;; esac
}
case "${PHASE:?export PHASE=start, accept, remote, terminal, blocked or abandon}" in
  start|accept|remote|terminal|abandon) worktree_ok= ;;
  blocked) worktree_ok="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1" ;;
  *) echo "unknown PHASE=$PHASE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
counter="/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-push-1056-$CARD-$PHASE.count"
porcelain=$(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all)
staged=$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3 { x = substr($0, 1, 1); if (x != " " && x != "?") print substr($0, 4) }')
test -z "$staged"
for path in $(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }'); do
  case " $worktree_ok " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "uncommitted change not allowed for PHASE=$PHASE: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
test "$(libra branch --show-current)" = main
SHA=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
test "$(libra rev-parse main)" = "$SHA"
obj=$(libra cat-file -p "$SHA")
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$obj" | grep -c '^parent ')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$obj" | grep -c '^gpgsig ')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$obj" | grep -c '^Signed-off-by: ')" -ge 1
changed=$(libra diff --name-only "$SHA^" "$SHA")
test -n "$changed"
test -z "$(printf '%s\n' "$changed" | grep -vxF -e "$plan" -e "$status_file" || true)"
parent=$(libra rev-parse "$SHA^")
libra fetch origin
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
if [ "$remote_main" != "$SHA" ] && [ "$parent" = "$remote_main" ]; then
  if [ "$PHASE" = start ]; then
    gate_script=$(mktemp /Volumes/Data/tmp/start-gate.XXXXXX)
    python3 -I - "$plan" "$gate_script" <<'GATEX'
import re, sys
text = open(sys.argv[1], encoding='utf-8').read()
hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^\*\*开工门（`bash` 运行', text)]
if len(hits) != 1:
    sys.exit('start gate anchor matched %d times' % len(hits))
fence = chr(96) * 3
m = re.compile('(?m)^' + fence + r'bash\n').search(text, hits[0])
end = text.find('\n' + fence + '\n', m.end() - 1)
open(sys.argv[2], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(text[m.end():end + 1])
GATEX
    bash "$gate_script"
  fi
  runs=$(gh run list --repo libra-tools/libra --branch main --limit 100 --json databaseId,status,workflowName,headSha --jq '.[] | select(.status != "completed") | select(.workflowName != "Cancel superseded main Actions") | "\(.databaseId) \(.headSha)"')
  busy=''
  for run_sha in $(printf '%s\n' "$runs" | awk 'NF == 2 { print $2 }' | sort -u); do
    run_obj=$(libra cat-file -p "$run_sha") || { echo "cannot read commit $run_sha of a run on main; fetch and rerun" >&2; exit 1; }
    run_subject=$(printf '%s\n' "$run_obj" | awk 'body && !seen { print; seen = 1 } /^$/ { body = 1 }')
    case "$run_subject" in *"(#1056)") ;; *) busy="$busy $run_sha" ;; esac
  done
  if [ -n "$busy" ]; then echo "CI of other commits is still running on main ($busy); pushing now would make cancel-superseded-main cancel it; wait until those runs complete, then rerun" >&2; exit 1; fi
  push_branch="plan-push-1056-$CARD"
  libra branch -f "$push_branch" "$SHA"
  set +e
  libra push origin "$push_branch:main"
  push_rc=$?
  set -e
  libra branch -D "$push_branch" || echo "warning: could not delete the local branch $push_branch" >&2
  echo "push exit code $push_rc; checking the remote" >&2
fi
libra fetch origin
latest=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$latest"
landed=yes
if [ "$latest" != "$SHA" ]; then landed=$(on_remote_main "$SHA"); fi
if [ "$landed" = yes ]; then
  rm -f "$counter"
  echo "pushed $SHA"
  exit 0
fi
if [ "$latest" = "$parent" ]; then
  echo "push failed while origin/main did not move: authentication, network or server error; not retrying (ER-09)" >&2
  exit 1
fi
parent_on_main=$(on_remote_main "$parent")
if [ "$parent_on_main" = no ]; then
  echo "the parent $parent is not on remote main (an unpushed commit such as the C-group commit); this script only pushes one plan commit on top of origin/main" >&2
  exit 1
fi
attempts=$(( $(cat "$counter" 2>/dev/null || echo 0) + 1 ))
if [ "$attempts" -gt 3 ]; then echo "origin/main advanced $attempts times; stop and report" >&2; exit 1; fi
rebase_opts=
if [ -n "$worktree_ok" ] && [ -n "$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3')" ]; then rebase_opts=--autostash; fi
if ! libra rebase $rebase_opts origin/main; then
  if libra rebase --abort; then
    echo "rebase stopped on a conflict and was aborted: rebase by hand (plan-status.md: take origin/main and replay only this plan's row), re-sign with libra commit --amend -S --no-edit, rerun the staging guard, then rerun this script" >&2
  else
    echo "rebase did not start (see the error above); nothing to abort" >&2
  fi
  exit 1
fi
echo "$attempts" > "$counter"
gpgsig_digest() { libra cat-file -p "$1" | awk '/^gpgsig /{ f = 1; print; next } f && /^ /{ print; next } f { exit }' | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1; }
libra commit --amend -S --no-edit
if [ "$(gpgsig_digest HEAD)" = "$(gpgsig_digest "$SHA")" ]; then echo "the replayed commit still carries the old signature; libra rebase copies gpgsig verbatim, so the amend did not re-sign" >&2; exit 1; fi
echo "rebased onto origin/main (attempt $attempts): rerun the post-commit range check (and for PHASE=start the external prerequisite check), then rerun this script" >&2
exit 3
```

### `COMPATIBILITY.md` 约束

只改 `COMPATIBILITY.md:240` 原文中的 `--prefix` 分句：从「not exposed (deferred)」改为支持的前缀规则、缺失前缀退出 128、无尾斜杠不是用法错误，并在描述单元格内、结尾的 `|` 之前追加精确句。分类行 `:634` 不动，不增删行。

### Fixture 与金样

Libra 测试 fixture 在隔离临时仓库中构造，内容与下表一致；测试把下表 OID 写成断言常量，普通 CI 不需要 Git 控制构建。构造方式分两类：

- **判定树对象是否已写入的 fixture**（AC3、AC4、AC26、AC27、AC38、AC42 用到的 F1B 与 F4）不经仓库索引：先用 `libra hash-object -w --stdin` 写入各 blob，再用 `libra update-index --index-file <临时索引> --add --cacheinfo <mode>,<oid>,<path>` 把条目写进仓库目录之外的临时索引（F4 的 stage 1–3 冲突条目用 `libra update-index --index-file <临时索引> --index-info`）；工作区不放同名文件，仓库自己的索引保持为空；被测命令一律带 `--index-file <临时索引>`。原因：Libra 的 Operation Log 在每条修改仓库状态的命令之后做快照，快照会把当前仓库索引写成树对象（`src/internal/operation/snapshot.rs:579` 的 `tree_from_index`；`write-tree` 在 `src/cli.rs:2070` 属仓库作用域，失败的调用也记一次），所以用 `libra add` 构造的 fixture 在运行 `write-tree` 之前就已含金样树，「运行前不可读」不成立，「运行后可读」空过。2026-11-07 用已安装的 Libra 0.30.157 实测：按此方式构造 F1B 后，金样根树 `f77ac8dd8b029c38b0575714b43d671c84b559a9` 与子树 `e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00` 在运行前 `cat-file -e` 都失败，`write-tree --index-file <临时索引>` 输出 `f77ac8dd…` 之后两者都可读。
- **只比较输出、退出码与稳定码的 fixture**（F1、F2、F3、F3B）可以用 `libra add`、`libra add -N`、`libra update-index --cacheinfo` 与 `libra update-index --index-info` 在仓库索引上构造。

**M0（I1056-01 开工登记之后、改动源码之前的第一步）：** 在隔离临时仓库按上述规则构造全部六个 fixture，核对根树等 OID 与下表一致；对 F1B 与 F4 另外断言运行前金样树 `cat-file -e` 失败；结果（命令、OID、退出码）写入「实施证据」。任何一项不一致就停止并修订计划，不能用不同 fixture 冒充对照。

| fixture | 内容 |
|---|---|
| F1 | `00/file`=`zero\n`、`a/b/file`=`nested\n`、`top`=`top\n`；gitlink `sub/link`=`1111…1111`（160000）；intent-to-add `ita/new` |
| F1B | 与 F1 相同的三个 blob，没有 gitlink 与 intent-to-add；只用于新鲜索引的失败副作用 |
| F2 | 只有 `001/file`=`one\n` |
| F3 | `00/file`=`zero\n`，另以 `--cacheinfo 100644,2222…2222,other/missing` 加一个不存在的 blob（前缀外） |
| F3B | `00/file`=`zero\n`，另加不存在的 `00/missing`（前缀内） |
| F4 | `00/file`=`zero\n`，另以 `--index-info` 给 `conflict/file` 写入 stage 1、2、3（内容 `base`、`ours`、`theirs`） |

2026-11-07 07:28:01 UTC 用本机固定 Git 控制（`/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-testcases/git-build-6de20f6092`，`git version 2.56.0.116.g6de20f6092`）执行下面的金样脚本，结果如下。这只验证计划命令与控制输出，不代表 M0 或产品验收。

| 场景 | Git 结果 |
|---|---|
| F1 `write-tree` | `9d531ae8a171150e1fb674f4cb3cd2bfb2b991ae`，退出 0 |
| F1 `--prefix=00/`、`--prefix=00` | 都是 `e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00` |
| F1 `--prefix=a/b`、`--prefix=a//b` | 都是 `f8afc4798ba81c152ca313148c665fd76d52ff1e` |
| F1 `--prefix=` | 与根树相同 |
| F1 `--prefix=/a`、`.`、`..`、`missing/`、`top` | 都退出 128，stderr 为 `fatal: git-write-tree: prefix <原文> not found` |
| F1 `--prefix=/`、`//a`、`./a`、`a/./b` | 都退出 128，`prefix <原文> not found`（2026-11-07 09:36 UTC 用本脚本重跑） |
| F1 `--prefix=ita/` | `4b825dc642cb6eb9a060e54bf8d69288fbee4904`（空树），退出 0 |
| F1 `--prefix=sub/` | `a8c7aeeba3eb6cb0bf20417a4debd717701ba142`，退出 0 |
| F1B `--prefix=missing/` 失败前后 | 同内容仓库 F1C 算出根树 `f77ac8dd8b029c38b0575714b43d671c84b559a9`、`00/` 子树 `e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00`；F1B 失败前 `cat-file -e` 根树退出 1，失败之后根树与子树的 `cat-file -e` 都退出 0（2026-11-07 09:36 UTC 用本脚本重跑） |
| F2 `--prefix=00` | 退出 128，`prefix 00 not found` |
| F3 `--prefix=00/` | 退出 128，`invalid object … for 'other/missing'` |
| F3 `--missing-ok --prefix=00/` | `e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00` |
| F3B `--missing-ok --prefix=00/` | `99b60eafbf00400e826f7684cafb1ab1fbe2b9bb` |
| F4 带前缀与不带前缀 | 都退出 128，stderr 含 `unmerged` 与 `error building trees`；带前缀被拒绝之后 `00/` 子树 `e9daf4a9…` 的 `cat-file -e` 退出 1（不写任何树）；Libra 不带前缀时的差异见 DEFER-I1056-03 |
| F1R（F1 同内容）`git replace` 根树为 `00/` 子树之后 `--prefix=00/` | 仍输出 `e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00`，退出 0（2026-11-07 10:43 UTC 用本脚本实测） |

金样脚本（`bash` 运行，参数为固定 Git 控制的 `bin-wrappers/git`）。F1B 副作用按以下方式取证：先在同内容的另一个仓库 F1C 算出根树与 `00/` 子树的 ID（`write-tree` 只写 F1C 自己的对象库），再在新鲜的 F1B 上确认失败前根树不存在（`sidefx-root-before` 退出非零）、`--prefix=missing/` 失败之后两者都可由 `cat-file -e` 读取：

```bash
set -eu
G="$1"
root=$(mktemp -d /Volumes/Data/tmp/i1056-golden.XXXXXX)
mkdir -p "$root/home"
export HOME="$root/home" GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null
"$G" --version
fixture() {
  local dir="$1"
  "$G" init -q --object-format=sha1 "$dir"
  "$G" -C "$dir" config user.name fixture
  "$G" -C "$dir" config user.email fixture@example.invalid
}
run() {
  local label="$1"; shift
  set +e
  out=$("$G" "$@" 2>"$root/err"); rc=$?
  set -e
  printf '%s rc=%s out=%s err=%s\n' "$label" "$rc" "$out" "$(tr '\n' ' ' < "$root/err")"
}
F1="$root/f1"; fixture "$F1"
mkdir -p "$F1/00" "$F1/a/b" "$F1/ita"
printf 'zero\n' > "$F1/00/file"; printf 'nested\n' > "$F1/a/b/file"; printf 'top\n' > "$F1/top"; printf 'new\n' > "$F1/ita/new"
"$G" -C "$F1" add 00/file a/b/file top
"$G" -C "$F1" update-index --add --cacheinfo 160000,1111111111111111111111111111111111111111,sub/link
"$G" -C "$F1" add -N ita/new
run fresh-missing -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=missing/
run root -C "$F1" write-tree
run p00slash -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=00/
run p00 -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=00
run ab -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=a/b
run abdouble -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=a//b
run empty -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=
run lead -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=/a
run dot -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=.
run dotdot -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=..
run slash -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=/
run dslash -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=//a
run dota -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=./a
run adotb -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=a/./b
run ita -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=ita/
run sub -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=sub/
run blob -C "$F1" write-tree --prefix=top
F1B="$root/f1b"; fixture "$F1B"
mkdir -p "$F1B/00" "$F1B/a/b"
printf 'zero\n' > "$F1B/00/file"; printf 'nested\n' > "$F1B/a/b/file"; printf 'top\n' > "$F1B/top"
"$G" -C "$F1B" add 00/file a/b/file top
F1C="$root/f1c"; fixture "$F1C"
mkdir -p "$F1C/00" "$F1C/a/b"
printf 'zero\n' > "$F1C/00/file"; printf 'nested\n' > "$F1C/a/b/file"; printf 'top\n' > "$F1C/top"
"$G" -C "$F1C" add 00/file a/b/file top
root_b=$("$G" -C "$F1C" write-tree)
sub_b=$("$G" -C "$F1C" write-tree --prefix=00/)
run sidefx-root-before -C "$F1B" cat-file -e "$root_b"
run sidefx-missing -C "$F1B" write-tree --prefix=missing/
run sidefx-root-after -C "$F1B" cat-file -e "$root_b"
run sidefx-sub-after -C "$F1B" cat-file -e "$sub_b"
F2="$root/f2"; fixture "$F2"
mkdir -p "$F2/001"; printf 'one\n' > "$F2/001/file"; "$G" -C "$F2" add 001/file
run boundary -C "$F2" write-tree --prefix=00
F3="$root/f3"; fixture "$F3"
mkdir -p "$F3/00"; printf 'zero\n' > "$F3/00/file"; "$G" -C "$F3" add 00/file
"$G" -C "$F3" update-index --add --cacheinfo 100644,2222222222222222222222222222222222222222,other/missing
run ext-missing -C "$F3" write-tree --prefix=00/
run ext-missing-ok -C "$F3" write-tree --missing-ok --prefix=00/
F3B="$root/f3b"; fixture "$F3B"
mkdir -p "$F3B/00"; printf 'zero\n' > "$F3B/00/file"; "$G" -C "$F3B" add 00/file
"$G" -C "$F3B" update-index --add --cacheinfo 100644,2222222222222222222222222222222222222222,00/missing
run int-missing-ok -C "$F3B" write-tree --missing-ok --prefix=00/
F4="$root/f4"; fixture "$F4"
mkdir -p "$F4/00"; printf 'zero\n' > "$F4/00/file"; "$G" -C "$F4" add 00/file
b1=$(printf 'base\n' | "$G" -C "$F4" hash-object -w --stdin); b2=$(printf 'ours\n' | "$G" -C "$F4" hash-object -w --stdin); b3=$(printf 'theirs\n' | "$G" -C "$F4" hash-object -w --stdin)
printf '100644 %s 1\tconflict/file\n100644 %s 2\tconflict/file\n100644 %s 3\tconflict/file\n' "$b1" "$b2" "$b3" | "$G" -C "$F4" update-index --index-info
run unmerged-prefixed -C "$F4" write-tree --prefix=00/
run unmerged-no-subtree -C "$F4" cat-file -e e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00
run unmerged-unprefixed -C "$F4" write-tree
F1R="$root/f1r"; fixture "$F1R"
mkdir -p "$F1R/00" "$F1R/a/b" "$F1R/ita"
printf 'zero\n' > "$F1R/00/file"; printf 'nested\n' > "$F1R/a/b/file"; printf 'top\n' > "$F1R/top"; printf 'new\n' > "$F1R/ita/new"
"$G" -C "$F1R" add 00/file a/b/file top
"$G" -C "$F1R" update-index --add --cacheinfo 160000,1111111111111111111111111111111111111111,sub/link
"$G" -C "$F1R" add -N ita/new
root_r=$("$G" -C "$F1R" write-tree)
sub_r=$("$G" -C "$F1R" write-tree --prefix=00/)
"$G" -C "$F1R" replace "$root_r" "$sub_r"
run replaced-root -C "$F1R" write-tree --prefix=00/
echo "root=$root"
```

### 控制组与 TC-0142（两门）

先执行前置块，再执行门块；每行是一个独立门。Git 轨必须 PASS。Libra 轨按结果表绑定唯一目标测试编号，拒绝缺行、SKIP、框架错误和异常标记，只接受该测试内恰好一次成功的精确 `git write-tree --prefix=00/`；后续 `mktree`/`archive` 失败如实记录，不把 TC-0142 记为 PASS。`PERL_TEST_HELPERS` 或 `ZIPINFO` 不可用会使用例 SKIP：作为 DEP-I1056-02 未满足处理，不作产品失败。

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases
test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = b8ad10bd14d95ffc03cd55927a37f31e67d8ff42
test -z "$(git status --porcelain)"
export CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra/target
export GIT_REF_REPO=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/git
export TC_TMP_ROOT=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-1056-tc
export GIT_BUILD_DIR="$TC_TMP_ROOT/git-build-6de20f6092"
bash cases/lib/build-git-control.sh
test "$("$GIT_BUILD_DIR/bin-wrappers/git" --version)" = "git version 2.56.0.116.g6de20f6092"
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
cargo build --bin libra
test "$(cargo metadata --no-deps --format-version 1 | jq -er .target_directory)" = /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra/target
test "$(target/debug/libra --version)" = "libra $(cargo metadata --no-deps --format-version 1 | jq -er '.packages[] | select(.name == "libra") | .version')"
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases
run_1056_git() {
  local output
  export TC_TRACK=git LIBRA_BIN="$GIT_BUILD_DIR/bin-wrappers/git"
  TC_RUN_ID=$(basename "$(mktemp -d "$TC_TMP_ROOT/i1056-git-XXXXXX")") || return 1
  export TC_RUN_ID
  output=$(bash cases/tc0123-0143-archive/tc0123-0143-archive.sh 0142) || return 1
  printf 'GIT %s\n' "$output"
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$output" | grep -cE '^TC-0142 PASS([ :]|$)')" -ge 1 ] || return 1
}
run_1056_libra() {
  local output rc out num
  export TC_TRACK=libra LIBRA_BIN=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra/target/debug/libra
  TC_RUN_ID=$(basename "$(mktemp -d "$TC_TMP_ROOT/i1056-libra-XXXXXX")") || return 1
  export TC_RUN_ID
  if output=$(bash cases/tc0123-0143-archive/tc0123-0143-archive.sh 0142); then rc=0; else rc=$?; fi
  printf '%s\n' "$output"
  [ "$rc" -eq 0 ] || [ "$rc" -eq 1 ] || return 1
  out="$TC_TMP_ROOT/runs/$TC_RUN_ID/libra/t5004-archive-corner-cases"
  [ -f "$out/results.tsv" ] || return 1
  [ -f "$out/cmdlog.tsv" ] || return 1
  [ -f "$out/markers.tsv" ] || return 1
  num=$(awk -F '\t' '$6 == "zip archive with many entries" {count++; n=$1; result=$2}
    END {if (count != 1 || (result != "ok" && result != "failure")) exit 1; print n}' "$out/results.tsv") || return 1
  awk -F '\t' -v n="$num" '$1 == "T" n && $4 == "git write-tree --prefix=00/" { count++; if ($2 != "0") bad=1 }
    END {exit !(count == 1 && !bad)}' "$out/cmdlog.tsv" || return 1
  awk -F '\t' -v n="$num" '$1 == "T" n {bad=1} END {exit bad}' "$out/markers.tsv" || return 1
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$output" | grep -cE '^TC-0142 (PASS|FAIL)([ :]|$)')" -ge 1 ] || return 1
}
```

门块：

```bash
set -eo pipefail
run_1056_git
run_1056_libra
```

计划阶段用合成 cmdlog 验证过该精确匹配会拒绝其他测试编号、非零退出和重复行；这不是 TC 验收。

| EX-I1056-01 | I1056-01 | G-03 门族型 AC / Verification 上限 | 同一「前缀子树」轴上的 53 个单一谓词判据（43 个 fixture 判据与 10 个逐文件文档语义判据；ER-06/06a 同步门及其精确句不计入）与 38 个具名门（26 个精确测试、文档门里 10 个计数的判定、TC-0142 两轨 2 门）；它是一份对照固定 Git 金样的 fixture 清单：每条判据都把 Libra 在「Fixture 与金样」表中某个 fixture 上的输出、退出码或稳定码，与表中记录的 Git 结果比对，属于模板列出的 fixture 清单型门族；每门都是判据规范块或「控制组与 TC-0142」门块里的可复制命令，失败即整卡不达标；一个测试可断言同一 fixture 上的多个谓词，映射见卡内表格；拆卡会割裂同一构建函数的实现与回归 | 待维护者具名批准，当前未批准 | 本轮计划审查待定 | 本卡；数量变化须重审 |

### Task I1056-01: 按前缀写子树

**Task type:** implementation

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** pending / 空

**Description:** 增加 `--prefix`，按 ADR-I1056-01 写出并打印前缀子树。

**Out of scope:** `mktree`、archive、commit 路径、无前缀入口的未合并行为（DEFER-I1056-03）。

**Current evidence:** `src/command/write_tree.rs:27-40,87`；`src/internal/tree_plumbing.rs:98-123,232-253,329-336`（只调用，不修改）；`src/command/reset.rs:1570-1575`；`src/command/mod.rs:249`；Git `builtin/write-tree.c:56,68`、`cache-tree.c:733-756,772-776`；「Fixture 与金样」。

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [ ] F1 上 `--prefix=00/` 的 stdout 等于金样 `e9daf4a92d968b17e551c6681c76c2864fa80e00`。
- [ ] 同一调用退出 0。
- [ ] 在 F1B 临时索引上（见「Fixture 与金样」的构造规则，运行前对象库里没有这棵树），`write-tree --index-file <临时索引> --prefix=00/` 返回的对象可由 `cat-file -e` 读取。
- [ ] 该对象的 `cat-file -t` 是 `tree`。
- [ ] `--prefix=00` 与 `--prefix=00/` 的 stdout 相同。
- [ ] `--prefix=a/b` 的 stdout 等于金样 `f8afc4798ba81c152ca313148c665fd76d52ff1e`。
- [ ] F2（只有 `001/file`）上 `--prefix=00` 以 128 退出。
- [ ] F1 上 `--prefix=missing/` 以 128 退出。
- [ ] 同一调用的 stderr 含原文 `missing/`。
- [ ] 同一调用的 stderr 含 `not found`。
- [ ] 同一调用的稳定码是 `LBR-REPO-003`。
- [ ] `--prefix=` 的 stdout 等于不带前缀时的根树（金样 `9d531ae8a171150e1fb674f4cb3cd2bfb2b991ae`）。
- [ ] `--prefix=` 退出 0。
- [ ] `--prefix=a//b` 与 `--prefix=a/b` 的 stdout 相同。
- [ ] `--prefix=/a` 以 128 退出。
- [ ] `--prefix=/` 以 128 退出（前导 `/` 先于折叠判定）。
- [ ] `--prefix=.` 以 128 退出。
- [ ] `--prefix=..` 以 128 退出。
- [ ] `--prefix=//a` 以 128 退出（前导 `/` 先于折叠判定）。

第二段，文档门（提交前于最终树执行）：

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
docs='docs/commands/write-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md docs/development/commands/write-tree.md'
if rg -n -e 'is not exposed \(deferred\)' -e 'not exposed \(deferred\)' -e '未公开（延后）' -e '未公开：Git 的 `--prefix' -e '`--prefix` \| 延后' $docs; then
  echo "stale deferred --prefix wording" >&2; exit 1
else
  rc=$?; test "$rc" -eq 1
fi
line240=$(sed -n 240p COMPATIBILITY.md)
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$line240" | grep -c '^| write-tree |')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$line240" | grep -cE 'prefix` not exposed|not exposed \(deferred\)')" = 0
readme_row=$(grep -F '| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' docs/development/commands/README.md)
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$readme_row" | grep -c .)" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$readme_row" | grep -c 'deferred')" = 0
readme_others_head=$(libra cat-file -p HEAD:docs/development/commands/README.md | grep -vF '| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |')
readme_others_now=$(grep -vF '| [`write-tree`](write-tree.md) |' docs/development/commands/README.md)
test "$readme_others_head" = "$readme_others_now"
sentence='With a prefix, write-tree validates the whole index and writes all of its trees, then prints the object ID of the subtree at that directory; a missing prefix fails with exit code 128.'
found=$(rg -l -F "$sentence" $docs | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
test "$found" = 3
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$line240" | grep -cF "$sentence")" = 1
syn_en=$(awk -v fence="$(printf '\140\140\140')" '/^## Synopsis$/ { f = 1; next } f && index($0, fence) == 1 { c++; if (c == 2) exit; next } f && c == 1' docs/commands/write-tree.md)
syn_zh=$(awk -v fence="$(printf '\140\140\140')" '/^## 用法$/ { f = 1; next } f && index($0, fence) == 1 { c++; if (c == 2) exit; next } f && c == 1' docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md)
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$syn_en" | grep -cF -- '--prefix=<prefix>')" -ge 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$syn_zh" | grep -cF -- '--prefix=<prefix>')" -ge 1
test "$(grep -cF -- '| `--prefix=<prefix>` |' docs/commands/write-tree.md)" -ge 1
test "$(grep -cF -- '| `--prefix=<prefix>` |' docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md)" -ge 1
exit_en=$(grep -F -- '| `128` |' docs/commands/write-tree.md)
exit_zh=$(grep -F -- '| `128` |' docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md)
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$exit_en" | grep -c .)" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$exit_zh" | grep -c .)" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$exit_en" | grep -cF 'LBR-REPO-003')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$exit_en" | grep -cF 'LBR-CONFLICT-001')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$exit_zh" | grep -cF 'LBR-REPO-003')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$exit_zh" | grep -cF 'LBR-CONFLICT-001')" = 1
test "$(grep -cF 'A missing prefix still leaves the trees written for the whole index in the object store.' docs/commands/write-tree.md)" -ge 1
test "$(grep -cF '前缀不存在时，为整个索引写出的树对象仍留在对象库中。' docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md)" -ge 1
before=$(libra cat-file -p HEAD:COMPATIBILITY.md | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
after=$(wc -l < COMPATIBILITY.md | tr -d ' ')
test "$before" = "$after"
```

ER-04 A 组执行 `cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra && source .env.test && cargo nextest run --no-tests=fail --test command_test -E 'test(/^command::write_tree_test::/)'`、`source .env.test && cargo nextest run --no-tests=fail --test compat_write_tree_missing_object`（`tests/INDEX.md:93` 钉住 `LBR-REPO-002` 的既有缺失对象契约）与 `source .env.test && cargo nextest run --no-tests=fail --test compat_ledger_schema`。C 组在 bump 后由 C 组运行器重跑判据规范第一段的 26 个测试门、文档门与 TC 两门，再执行 C 组脚本。不可把其他测试的成功替代缺失的具名门。

**Full-suite trigger:** T-1：patch bump 改写 `install.sh` 与 `install.ps1`。C 组脚本执行 `cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra && source .env.test && source .env.live-test && cargo nextest run --all --no-fail-fast --retries 2`，focused 不能代替。

**Dependencies:** DEP-I1056-SCAN（开工门写者扫描）、DEP-I1056-01、DEP-I1056-02、DEP-I1056-03、DEP-I1056-05、DEP-I1056-06；DEP-I1056-04 不阻塞。 **Deliverables:** N/A。

**Implementation write set:** `src/command/write_tree.rs`、`tests/command/write_tree_test.rs`、`docs/commands/write-tree.md`、`docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md`、`docs/development/commands/write-tree.md`、`docs/development/commands/README.md`（只改 write-tree 行）、`COMPATIBILITY.md`（第 240 行）、`/Volumes/Sea/libra-backend/apps/tanstack-app/content/docs/commands/write-tree.en.md`（同步脚本只生成这一页，zh-CN 路由渲染同一页）。

**Release write set:** `Cargo.toml`、`install.sh`、`install.ps1`、工具链刷新的 `Cargo.lock`、tag `v<version>`、GitHub Release 与 release artifact；窗口见 DEP-I1056-06。 **Files likely touched:** 同写集，另加本文件与 `plan-status.md` 本计划行。

**Docs and compatibility impact:** `COMPATIBILITY.md:240` 的 `--prefix` 分句改为支持的前缀规则、缺失前缀为 128、无尾斜杠不是用法错误，并说明只有带前缀的入口拒绝未合并索引；`docs/commands/write-tree.md`、`docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md`、`docs/development/commands/write-tree.md` 同样写明；EN 与 zh-CN 命令文档的 Synopsis / 用法块（`docs/commands/write-tree.md:10`、`docs/commands/zh-CN/write-tree.md:8`）加入 `--prefix=<prefix>`，选项表（EN `:40-44`、zh-CN `:30-34`）加一行 `--prefix=<prefix>`，删除「未公开（延后）」句（EN `:46`、zh-CN `:36`），退出码表的 `128` 行（EN `:55`、zh-CN `:43`）列出 `LBR-REPO-003`（前缀不存在）与 `LBR-CONFLICT-001`（带前缀时索引未合并），并各写一行失败副作用句（AC44–AC53）；三份文档各自单独成行写入精确句 `With a prefix, write-tree validates the whole index and writes all of its trees, then prints the object ID of the subtree at that directory; a missing prefix fails with exit code 128.`；网站页由同步脚本生成。`docs/development/commands/README.md:81` 的 write-tree 行把「`--prefix`/`--missing-ok` deferred」改为当前事实（`--prefix` 写子树；`--missing-ok` 已随 PD-05 落地）。无新错误码，`docs/error-codes.md` 与 `tests/INDEX.md` 为 N/A。

**Rollback mode:** `immutable-release`：tag、GitHub Release 与 release artifact 一旦建立就不撤回，修复只发新的 patch 版本前滚（生命周期第 6 步）；C 组提交推送之前的撤销按生命周期第 9 步放弃，不是第二种恢复模式。降级指引：用户回装上一个 tag 的发布物（`curl -fsSL https://download.libra.tools/install.sh | sh -s -- --version v<上一版本>`，Windows 用 `install.ps1 -Version v<上一版本>`，或设置 `LIBRA_VERSION`）。兼容窗口：本卡不改仓库格式、数据库 schema 或对象格式，上一版本与本版本读写同一仓库，降级不需要迁移，只有本卡改变的命令行为回到旧行为。降级路径由发布四段第四段的降级演练实际验证：把比本版本低的最新正式发布版本装进临时目录并核对 `--version`，结果写入 `<CARD>:ROLLBACK_VERIFIED=`，终止链的暂存守卫要求这一项。 **Migration and rollback:** N/A。 **Security and privacy:** 前缀按路径分量匹配，不按字节前缀，因此 `00` 不命中 `001`。 **Performance budget:** 对象校验与根树写入只做一次（与无前缀入口相同），之后按前缀目录层数读取若干树对象（ADR 第 6 步），不重建子树，不跳过前缀外对象校验。 **Estimated scope:** M。 **Version increment:** patch。 **Release boundary:** independent。 **C/D coverage from:** self。

**Granularity:** type=implementation; axis=prefix subtree; recovery=immutable-release; complete=yes; self-contained=yes; AC=53/8@EX-I1056-01; VER=38/8@EX-I1056-01; landing=1; prod-files=1; scope=M; deps=DEP-I1056-SCAN,DEP-I1056-01,DEP-I1056-02,DEP-I1056-03,DEP-I1056-05,DEP-I1056-06; writeset=DEP-I1056-03; release=independent; split-from=N/A; exception=EX-I1056-01 待批准。

## 里程碑验收与回滚

A/B、Codex ER-05 PASS、C、`remote-pending`、D 全绿、终止链提交之后才算 `done`/`complete`，见「生命周期」。卡的 `done` 不表示 TC-0142 全绿，也不关闭 #1056，除非 DEP-I1056-04 的两个命令都已交付且 TC 随后 PASS。

### 故障恢复矩阵

| 事件 | 动作 |
|---|---|
| 前缀测试失败 | 修复后重跑，不记 PASS |
| Libra fixture 的 OID 与金样不一致 | 停止并修订计划 |
| TC 仍报未知前缀 | 本卡未完成 |
| TC 因 mktree/archive 失败 | 记录并保持 DEP-I1056-04，不扩大本卡 |
| 上游前进 | 开工前：开工检查在干净工作区里 `libra pull --ff-only`；bump 前：`libra pull --ff-only --autostash`（只涉及本卡改动）；C 组提交之后：发布四段开头的有界恢复（rebase、重签，上游有任何增量都整套重跑 C 组，最多 3 次）；开工登记、`remote-pending` 登记或终止链提交：计划目录推送脚本的有界恢复（最多 3 次） |
| 工作区出现本卡允许集合以外的改动 | 工作区独占检查失败，停止；不提交、移动或清理他人改动；开工前保持 `pending`；开工后按 ER-10 停止并报告，不做 `blocked` 登记（登记提交无法与他人改动隔离），本卡保持 `in-progress`，对其它计划的开工门同样算活动写者 |
| 外部前置长期不可用，或用户决定改期 | 本卡没有推送过 C 组提交时按生命周期第 9 步放弃：撤销本卡改动，回到 `pending` / 空，释放家族串行锁；已推送的卡不能放弃，只能修复或前滚 |
| 推送报错 | 先看远端：fetch 后 `origin/main` 等于该 SHA 或 GitHub compare 显示它已在 `main` 上，就算推送成功，原样重跑脚本即可确认；`origin/main` 未动是认证、网络或服务端错误，按 ER-09 不重试，记录错误后报告；已前进到别处进入有界恢复 |
| 候选分支 CI 重跑一次后在建 tag 之前仍失败 | 先按 ER-13 在本地复现并归因。与本卡有关时进入生命周期第 6 步修复模式：不 bump，在本卡写集内修复，重走 C 组与第一段（`MODE=repair`，修复 SHA 快进推上同一候选分支），在修复 SHA 上建原版本的 tag；与本卡无关时保持 `in-progress` / `remote-pending` 并向用户报告，不在写集外修改 |
| C 组本地门失败（Verification 门、fmt、Clippy、doc、全量 nextest 或 release 构建） | 先归因：本卡引入的就在写集内修复，再由 C 组运行器整套重跑；在 bump 前的基线上也能复现的既有失败，按 ER-13 登记 FIX- 或 DEFER- 并向用户报告，不在写集外修改，本卡保持 `in-progress` / `locally-accepted`，需要等待时按生命周期第 8 步登记 `blocked` |
| 网站段一在 `--write` 之后失败（类型检查、构建或页面核对） | 后端工作区留下本卡命令页的生成改动：只用 `libra restore` 还原这些 `apps/tanstack-app/content/docs/commands/<命令>.en.md` 页面，确认后端工作区干净后修正原因再重跑段一；失败的生成结果不提交、不推送 |
| 版本面冲突的 rebase 恢复 | `libra reset --mixed HEAD^` 撤销未推送的 C 组提交并清空暂存区，四个版本面文件恢复为原基线后从生命周期第 3 步重来；工作区独占检查断言暂存区为空，所以漏掉这一步会在 C 组之前就失败 |
| 建 tag 之后 release 失败 | tag 不改写；先删除该版本的候选分支（尖端必须是本卡 `PUSHED_SHA`，删除后回读为 404），再在已有 tag 的版本之上 bump 到下一版本前滚修复，新版本用新的候选分支 |
| 第三段 `gh release create` 报错或建 tag 后中断 | 原样重跑第三段：远端 tag 已存在且指向本卡 SHA、对应 Release 也存在时只补记 `TAG` 并继续第四段；tag 指向别的提交，或只有 tag 没有 Release，都停止并报告，不改写 tag |
| 第一段之前的外部前置检查失败 | C 组提交已在本地、尚未推送：只停止并报告，不做 `blocked` 登记（计划目录推送脚本也会拒绝父提交不在远端 `main` 上的登记提交）；前置恢复后重跑检查与第一段 |
| 网站段二推送报错或提交后中断 | 原样重跑段二：已有本卡网站提交（工作区干净、`HEAD` 的提交说明是本卡的同步说明）时不再提交，`origin/cf` 已等于它即视为推送成功；`origin/cf` 前进到别处时停止并报告：以 `origin/cf` 为底重新生成本卡页面，用 `libra commit --amend -S --no-edit` 重签（`libra rebase` 逐字节复制旧签名头，复制来的签名已失效；段二重跑时核对父提交变化时签名块也已变），取得新的审查 PASS 后再重跑 |
| 第三段发现更高版本 tag 或 CDN 已更新 | 不建 tag；本卡版本号作废并记入「实施证据」，在新基线上以新的 C 组提交前滚 |
| 候选分支 run 被取消或失败 | 第二段只看 `release/v<version>` 上本卡 SHA 的 run：被取消时 `gh run rerun` 一次，失败时 `gh run rerun --failed` 一次；重跑后仍不是 success 转修复模式（生命周期第 6 步）；超时保持 `in-progress` / `remote-pending`，原样重跑第二段继续等待 |
| 推送 `main` 前远端 `main` 上有其它提交的在途 run | 发布第一段或计划目录推送脚本停止，不推送（推送会让 `cancel-superseded-main` 取消它们，被取消的 run 不算 PASS）；等那些 run 结束后原样重跑；`gh` 查询或读取提交失败同样停止，恢复后重跑 |
| 第四段的降级演练失败 | 候选分支已删除、`RELEASE_RUN` 已写入，卡保持 `in-progress` / `remote-pending`，不写 `ROLLBACK_VERIFIED`；第四段可原样重跑（release run 与候选分支只核对，已删除的候选分支不再删除），按报错修正前置（网络、`/Volumes/Data/tmp` 空间、上一发布版本的 artifact）后重跑；演练 `PATH` 仍能找到 `libra` 或安装器未写入演练目录时停止，查明原因后重跑 |

## 风险登记

| 风险 | 处理 |
|---|---|
| 改到 commit 共用函数 | 本卡不改 `tree_plumbing.rs`，只调用公开函数；A 组的 `command::write_tree_test` 全模块与 `compat_write_tree_missing_object`、C 组全量覆盖根树与 commit 路径 |
| 尾斜杠可有可无 | 不是用法错误，与 Git 分量查找一致 |
| 前缀子树与根树中的子树不一致 | 前缀子树直接从同一次写出的根树中逐级取出，不重建；AC1 与副作用判据（F1B）共同约束 |

## 性能与容量摘要

对象校验与根树构建各只做一次，之后按前缀目录层数逐级读取树对象，不重建子树；不以提前过滤取代整索引验证。

## 兼容与文档收口

只改 `:240` 的前缀分句。

## 实施证据

尚未执行。按「生命周期」写入 C 组收据、TC-0142 两轨结果与 D 收据。
